piṟakkamuṟṟa malināṭu nāṭiyakaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ
peyartir mātō,
teṉtamiḻnāṭṭakaṉpotiyilṟirumuṉivaṉtamiḻccaṅkamcērkiṟpīrēl
eṉṟumavaṉuṟiviṭamām.........kāṇṭir’ 
(kiṭkintā, nāṭaviṭṭa paṭalam. 30.31.)
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.
icceyyuḷaṭikaḷil cōṇāṭṭuk kampar, cōḻa, cēra nāṭukaḷit tamiḻt toṭarpu kuṟiyāmal, vāḷā cōṇāṭu malināṭu eṉa avvan nāṭṭup peyarāṟ cuṭṭuvatum, pāṇṭi nāṭṭip pāṇṭi nāṭeṉṉāmal tamiḻnāṭu teṉtamiḻnāṭu eṉavē cuṭṭuvatum, pāṇṭināṭu jmiḻeṭeṉṟaṟkucáṟappurimiyuṭiyateṉpatuntarntubtaḷiyappeṟṟabcaytivṉpatitliyuṟuttum .iṉi, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṟkiluḷḷatoru pakutikku vaṭavelliyāka iruntateṉṟu vēṉiṟkātiyuriyil aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟutalāṉ aṟikiṉṟōm.
avar (pāṇṭiyar) nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭa velli yākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum iṭiyē, eḻunūṟṟukkāvatavāṟuṅ kaṭalkoṇ ṭoḻitalāl, toṭiyōḷ natiyu meṉṉātu, kumariyākiya puva meṉṟār eṉṟuṇarka eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutum urik kuṟippākum.
itil. paḵṟuḷiyiyum kumariyiyum pāṇṭiyariṉ kaṭal koṇṭa teṉpāli nilattiṉ irupērelliyāka orucērak kuṟittu, paḵṟuḷiyi, mutalūḻiyiṟutiyiṟ kaṭalkoṇṭa avartam teṉpālimukattiṉ vaṭavelliyeṉa viḷakki, kumariyi iṭam kuṟiyātu vaḷākūṟiyuḷḷār.ivvurik kuṟippiṟ kūṟappaṭum kumari paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟukku vaṭakkōṭiṟṟā? aṉṟit teṟkōṭiṟṟā? eṉpatu īṇṭu ārāyntu tuṇiyattakum.
aiyaravarkaḷ tam arumpata vakarātik kuṟippil paḵṟuḷi kumarikkut teṟkiṉkaṇ ōṭiyatōrāṟu eṉa varintataṟkut tām kaṇṭa ātāram yāteṉa viḷakkiṉārkaḷilli. avarkaḷ kūṟi yāṅkuk kumariyiṉ teṟkē paḵṟuḷi yiruntatāyiṉ, teṟkelli paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉāmal ataṉ vaṭakkōṭiya kumariyāṟṟik kūṟiyatu uṇmikku muraṇāvati yeṭuttukkāṭṭi, avarkaḷ koḷkik kuriya ātaravi viḷakki eṉ aiyamakaṟṟumāṟu avarkaḷukku yāṉ kaṭitameḻuti vēṇṭiṉēṉ.
vēṉiṟkātiyil aṭiyārkku nallār tarum urik kuṟippiyum tammāl tiṭṭamākac colla muṭiyāta ētō ātārattiyum koṇṭu paḵṟuḷiyik kumarikkut teṟkiṉkaṇ uḷḷatākat tām appōtu karutiyatāyum, āṉāl ataṉ uṇmi ārāyntu teḷiyavēṇṭiya toṉṟākumeṉavum aiyaravarkaḷ eṉakku aṟivittārkaḷ.
avarkaḷ karuttukkuriya merumatippi naṉkuṇarnta yāṉ palavāṟu turuvi ivviru yāṟṟiṉiyum paṟṟiya paḻañceyyuṭ kaḷiṉ kuṟippukkaḷi yellām ārāyntu pārttēṉ. ak kuṟippukkaḷellām paḵṟuḷiyik kumariyiṉ teṟkuḷḷatāka yāṇṭum eṉittaḷavum cuṭṭumāṟilli. ataṟku māṟākak kumariyiṉ vaṭakkiruntateṉak koḷḷavikkuṅ kuṟippukkaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aṭiyārkku nallārkkuk kālattāṉ muntiya cilappatikāra arumpatavuriyāciriyar, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kumarippakkattatōr yāṟu eṉak kūṟiṉāraṉṟik kumarikku eppakkattatu eṉak kuṟittārilli.
aṭiyārkku nallārō, pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ teṉpāli mukattu vaṭavelli paḵṟuḷi yeṉac cuṭṭi, appaḵṟuḷikkum kumariyāṟṟukku miṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavaḷavuḷḷa pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻnāṭṭut teṉpakuti mutalūḻiyiṟutiyiṟ kaṭalkoṇṭaḻintatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
mutalūḻiyil pulavar puṇar kūṭṭuṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṟ pāṇṭiyar tamiḻvaḷartta talinakaram kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa teṉmaturi eṉak kaḷaviyaluriyil nakkīrarum, cilappatikāra vuriyil aṭiyārkku nallārum maturik kāñciyil marutaṉārum kūṟutalāl, mutalūḻiyiṟutiyiṟ kaṭalkoṇṭaḻintatu meṉmaturi yuḷḷiṭṭa pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyeṉpatu tēṟṟamākum kaḷaviyaluriyit taḻuviyē tām eḻutuvatāka aṭiyārkku nallārum cuṭṭukiṉṟār.
ivviru perum pulavaruriyālum, piṟa cila kuṟippukkaḷālum appōtu aḻinta nāṭṭiṉaḷavu eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatameṉṟum, ataṉ vaṭakkum teṟkumākiya īrellikaḷ paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum eṉavum aṟiyalām.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭil mutalūḻi yiṟutiyil aḻinta teṉpāli nilattiṉ vaṭavelli paḵṟuḷiyeṉa viḷakkum aṭiyārkku nallār kuṟippil, kumari teṟkelliyeṉṟu kūṟappaṭāviṭiṉum, aḻinta nilattiṉ maṟṟōrelliyeṉac cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum īrelliyāka, iṭippaṭṭa nāṭu kaṭalkoṇṭateṉṟum, annilattiṉ vaṭavelli paḵṟuḷi yeṉṟum kuṟikkappeṟutalāl, ataṉ teṟkelli kumari yeṉpatu kūṟāmalē tēṟappaṭumaṉṟō?
itaṉāl, aḻinta tamiḻnilattiṉ vaṭavelli paḵṟuḷiyum ataṉ teṟkelli taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅkōṭum eṉa aṭiyārkku nallār karutiṉār eṉpatu orutali. aṭiyārkku nallāriṉ ikkaruttu, tolkāppiyap pāyiram tanta paṉampāraṉār, kuṟuṅkōḻiyūrkiḻār, māṅkuṭi marutaṉār mutaliya paḻamperum pulavarkaḷum, iḷampūraṇar mutaliya pēruriyāciriyarkaḷum kumariyip paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṟkelliyākavē orupaṭiyākak kūṟiya koḷkiyōṭu poruntiyamiyum cevviyum iṅkuc cintikkattakkatu.
kumari teṉkaṭal koṇṭaḻinta pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ teṉkaṭi yelliyāyiṉ, avvāṟaḻinta nilapparappik kumarimutal paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṉkāṟum eṉak kūṟuvatē muṟiyāmaṉṟi, vaṭa paḵṟuḷi mutal teṉkumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakari variyeṉak kūṟutal amiyātu; iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, aṭiyārkku nallārum paḵṟuḷiyi mutaliṟ kūṟi, avvāṟṟuṭaṉ kumariyaḷavum kaṭal koṇṭa teṉavē cuṭṭutalāl, paḵṟuḷi kumariyiṉ teṟkōṭiya teṉak koḷḷuvatē poruttamākum eṉa vātittal kūṭum. avvāṟu koṇṭāl, paḵṟuḷimutal kumarivariyeṉpatē amiyumātaliṉ taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅkōṭu eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟa kūṟṟākum. teṟkiruntu vantaḻikkum kaṭal, kumariyiṉ teṉkariyit toṭāmal vaṭakkē maṭṭuma aḻittuppōtal karutoṇātu. aṉṟiyum, kumarikku vaṭakari kūṟuvatāyiṉ paḵṟuḷiyiṉ teṉkōṭutoṭṭuk kumariyiṉ vaṭakōṭṭaḷavum eṉak kūṟal vēṇṭuma. avvāṟu kūṟāmal kumarikkumaṭṭum vaṭakari kuṟikkac ciṟanta kāraṇam vēṇṭum.
kumariyē teṟkelliyāyp paḵṟuḷi ataṉ vaṭakkē pāṇṭiyartam tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṭi yoḻukiya yāṟāmēlmaṭṭum, aṭiyārkku nallār kumariyiṉ vaṭakkelliyi, aḻinta tamiḻnilattiṉ ōrelliyākak kuṟittatu poruttamākum.
kumariyāṟē tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṟkelliyākavē, ataṉ teṟkē nāṭiruttalum atu tamiḻnāṭākāmal piṟanāṭātalum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. yāṟetaṟkum irukariyum irupuṟa nilapparappum iruttal vēṇṭum. orupuṟam nilamum maṟṟoru pakkam kaṭalumāy yāṟoḻukal kūṭāmi veḷippaṭi.
kumariyiṉ teṟkil piṟanāṭu uṇmiyāl atu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum piṟanāṭṭiṟkum varappaṟukkum elliyākak kūṟap paṭṭatu eṉpati iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
kaṭal koḷvataṉ muṉpu (tamaḻ nāṭṭiṟkut teṟkē) piṟanāṭum uṇmiyiṉ teṟkum elli kūṟappaṭṭatu; kiḻakkum mēṟkum (kaṭalkaḷēyaṉṟip) piṟanāṭiṉmiyiṉ (elli) kūṟap paṭāvāyiṉa eṉpataṉāl, kaṭal koṇṭateṉat tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum ataṉ teṟkē muṉ iruntu akkaṭalkōḷāl aḻinta piṟa nāṭṭiṟkum iṭiyē avaṟṟip pirittuniṉṟa elli kumariyāṟu eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭuvatōṭu, tamiḻ nilam akkumariyiṉ vaṭakari yaḷavē niṉṟatu eṉpatum viḷakkamākum. eṉavē, mutalūḻi yiṟutiyil aḻinta tamiḻ nāṭṭup pakutiyiṉ vaṭavelli paḵṟuḷi yeṉpatum, ataṉ teṟkelli kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyeṉpatum teḷivām.
ivvaḻivik kuṟikkum cilappatikārac ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ avaṟṟi yākkiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ nōkkattic cevvitiṟ pulappaṭutti niṟkiṉṟaṉa. aṭiyārkku nallārum, avarukkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉpirunta iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum kuṟippatu, avviruvarukkum palappala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ kaṭal kōḷāl aḻintoḻinta pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻnāṭṭup pakutiyākum, avvāṟaḻinta nilattaḷavu kūṟutaṟkup paḵṟuḷiyiyum kumariyiyum iraṇṭelliyāka avar koṇṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟākiya paḵṟuḷi appōtaḻinta pāṇṭiyariṉ teṉpāli nilattiṉ vaṭavelli yātaliṉ, ataṉ irupuṟamum iruntatu tamiḻ nāṭēyākum. kumariyāṟṟiṟkō ataṉ vaṭakkil tamiḻ nāṭum teṟkil piṟa nāṭumē yiruntaṉa.
aḻintataṉ vaṭakkēyuḷḷa piṟkālat tamiḻ nilattup pulavar, teṟkē muṉ aḻinta nilattik kuṟikkuṅkāl, taṅkaḷukku aṇittāyirunta paḵṟuḷiyic cuṭṭi, atumutal ataṉ teṟkē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ teṉkaṭi yelliyākiya kumari vaṭakarivari yirunta paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakuti nilam mutalūḻi yiṟutiyiṟ kaṭalkōḷāl aḻintatu eṉak kuṟippatu iyaṟkiyum poruttamumākum.
kumariyiṉ vaṭakariyaḷavē pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻnāṭum ataṉ teṉkari toṭṭup piṟanāṭum iruntataṉālum, piṟa nāṭṭaḻivaḷavu cuṭṭutal avar ceyyuḷil vēṇṭappaṭāmi yālum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ aḻivoṉṟē kūṟavanta avviru perum pulavarum kumariyiṉ vaṭakariyi appōtaḻinta pāṇṭit teṉṉilappakutikkut teṟkelliyākac cuṭṭi, amivupeṟak kūṟiṉar eṉat terikiṉṟōm.
iṉi, kumari muṉiyiliruntu immaṇṇulakiṉ teṉ turuvamē ēḻāyira milukkuṭpaṭṭirukkak kaṭal koṇṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉaḷavumaṭṭumē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatameṉpatu nakikkiṭaṉāy uṇmikku māṟupaṭuṅ kūṟṟākumeṉat taṟkāla ārāycciyāḷar cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
mutalūḻiyiṟutiyil kaṭal koṇṭa tamiḻ nilamē kumari muṉiyi yaṭuttaṉṟu ataṉ, teṟkē neṭuntūrattiliruntu iraṇṭā mūḻiyiṟutiyil aḻinta kapāṭapurattit talinakaramākak koṇṭa tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ parappiṟkum teṟkē, paḵṟuḷimutaṟ kumarivariyirunta oru pakutiyē mutalūḻiyiṟutiyil aḻintateṉpatu tamiḻppulavar kūṟum marapu. ātaliṉ, kumarimuṉi mutaṟ paḵṟuḷi variyirunta nilattaḷavum, paḵṟuḷi mutal ataṉ teṟkē kumari vari yiruntaḻinta eḻunūṟṟukkāvatat tamiḻ nilattaḷavum, mutali
laḻinta kumariyiṉ teṟkēyiruntoḻinta piṟanāṭṭiṉaḷavum, aviyaṉittiṟkum teṟkē avaṟṟik koṇṭaḻitta teṉ kaṭaṟ parappaḷavum kūṭṭiṉ, oru eḻunūṟōṭu nillātu, eṇṇiṟanta pala eḻunūṟṟuk kāvataṅkaḷ eṇṇal vēṇṭum. oru kāvatattiṉ aḷavu pattu milākak koṇṭālum, ivvaḷavu palap pala āyiram milkaḷākumātalāl, innilaṅkaḷellām pulavar paṭittuk kūṟum puṉinturiyāvataṉṟi, uṇmiyātal ollā teṉa ārāycciyāḷar eṭuttukkāṭṭi nakikkiṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu kūṟupavarkaḷ, oru nilattiṉaḷavu ataṉ nīḷattiyum akalattiyum perukkiya parappaḷavāvataṉṟi, akalatti aṟavē maṟantu nīḷattiṉ taṉiyaḷavāl oru nilattik kuṟittal iyaṟki yaṉṟu eṉpati maṟantu pēcuvatu viyappaṉṟō! tamiḻnāṭṭil eḻunūṟṟukkāvatam aḻittateṉṟāl, aḻinta anta nāṭṭukku akalam oṉṟum illāmal ūciya vaṭiyi viḷḷa ataṉūṭu tōṉṟum iḻipōla ēḻāyiram mil nīḷamaṭṭuḷḷa nilam eṉpatu poruḷāmā? aḻintatu oru nāṭu. eṉavē, atu akalamaṟṟa oru neṭiya kōṭu eṉak koḷḷutal amiyātākalāṉum, nāṭṭiṟku nīḷattōṭu akalamum vēṇṭappaṭumātalāṉum, av vīraḷavum perukkiya caturattaḷavē nilattaḷavāmātalāṉum, eḻunūṟṟukkāvatam allatu ēḻāyiram caturamil parappē mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷāl aḻinta tamiḻ nilappakutiyiṉ aḷavu eṉak koḷḷu talil viyappum aiyamum eḻuvataṟkilli.
ēṟattāḻa oru māvaṭṭa aḷavuḷḷa cērar malināṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa taṟkālat tiruvitāṅkūr varippu maṭṭumē 6000 catura milākiṟatu eṉa aṟiyum nām, cilappatikāram kuṟikkum ēḻāyiram caturamil aḷavuḷḷa oru tamiḻnilappakuti mutalūḻiyiṟutiyiṟ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭu aḻintatuṭaṉ, ataṉ piṟakē mīṇṭum maṟṟoru kaṭalkōḷāl mullikkali kuṟikkum pāṇṭiyariṉ iṭiyūḻik kapāṭapura nilapparappum aḻivuṟṟatu eṉpatil, aḷavoṉṟē koṇṭu ataṉi maṟuttaṟkuriya taṭiyetuvum kāṇutaṟkilli.
mutalūḻiyil kaṭalkōḷāl aḷavaṟiyāta piṟa teṉṉāṭ ṭuṭaṉ aḻinta tamiḻ nilapparappu, teṟkē kumariyiṉ vaṭakari mutal ataṉ vaṭakkē paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaḷavum eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatam allatu ēḻāyiram caturamil aḷavu eṉpatum, annilattiṉ kiḻakkum mēṟkum muṉ kaṭalkaḷē elliyāyiruntaṉa eṉpatum, aḻiyumuṉ kumarikkut teṟkē tamiḻ vaḻaṅkāta piṟa nāṭē yiruntateṉpatum, ammutaṟ kaṭalkōḷāl aḻintavi appiṟa nāṭum pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil kumarimutal paḵṟuḷivari yuḷḷa teṉpakutimaṭṭumē yeṉpatum, aṭiyārkku nallāruri yālum maṟṟum paḻam pulavarkaḷ kūṟum ceytikaḷālum oruṅkē teḷintu tuṇiyappaṭum uṇmikaḷākum.
mutalil aḻintatu pāṇṭiyar nāṭeṉṉātu avar nāṭṭut teṉpālimukam eṉappaṭutalāṉ, atu akkālap pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ teṉpakuti nilattiyē cuṭṭuvatu veḷippaṭi. annilamiḻanta pāṇṭiyar iraṇṭām ūḻiyil ataṉ vaṭakkuḷḷatāy aḻiyā teñciya tam nāṭṭuḷ, kapāṭapurattit talinakarākkik koṇṭu atiṟ pulavarik kūṭṭit tamiḻ pēṇum taṅkaḷ aṟamarapu pōṟṟi vantār eṉpatu, nakkīrar kaḷaviyaluriyālum, aṭiyārkku nallārurik kuṟippālum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kapāṭapuramē pāṇṭiyariṉ iṭiyūḻit talinakarameṉpatu, mikap paḻami kūṟappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyil āti kaviyākiya vāṉmīkar, cītiyit teṟkē tēṭaviṭuttataṉ tāṉit talivarukkuc cukkirīvaṉ cuṭṭiya ellik kuṟippāka viḷakkum vaṭamoḻi irāmāyaṇap pakutiyāl valipeṟukiṉṟatu.
acōkaṉukku muṉ pāṭalipurattil ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil āṇṭa cantirakupta cakkaravarttiyiṉ amiccaṉākiya cāṇakkiyaṉ iyaṟṟiya artta cātirattil mukattik kapāṭakam eṉac cuṭṭum kuṟippālum ivvuṇmi teḷiyappaṭum.
poruḷkaḷukkuc cuviyuṇṭāku miṭaṅkaḷāl peyaramivatu iyalpu. māyūrattiṉ oru pakutiyākiya korunāṭṭil neyyap paṭum cēlikaḷ korunāṭu eṉappaṭutalum, irāyapuriyiṟ ceyyappaṭum carukkari irāyapuri yeṉappaṭutalum, māmpaḻaṅkaḷ cēlam, paṅkaḷur eṉa aviyavi uṟpattiyākum ūrp peyar koṇṭu vaḻaṅkappaṭutalum, iṉṉa piṟavum ivvuṇmiyi viḷakkum.
paḻaṅkālattup pāṇṭiyariṉ teṉkaṭaloṉṟilaṉṟi yāṇṭum muttuṇṭāṉatilli eṉpatu ulaka caritam kūṟum uṇmiyākum. appāṇṭiyariṉ talinakarāṉa kapāṭapuratti liruntu pāṭalipurattil vantu viṟkappaṭṭa tamiḻk kaṭalpaṭu poruḷākiya muttukkuk kaṭaṟṟoṭarpēyillāta āriyariṉ naṭunāṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyil muṉ pērillāmiyāl atit taṅkaḷ nāṭṭukku mutalil aṉuppiya pāṇṭiyariṉ pērūrākiya kapāṭa purattāl kapāṭakam eṉap peyariṭṭatu veḷippaṭi. piṟaku nāḷaṭivil ataṉ iyaṟkit tamiḻppeyar muttu eṉa aṟinta piṉ appeyarit tirittu muktā eṉat taṅkaḷ moḻiyil āriyar peyariṭṭukkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu, cāṇakkiyaṉ nūlil appeyar kūṟappaṭāmal muttik kapāṭakam eṉṟē kūṟutalāṉum, cāṇakkiyaṉukkup piṟkāla nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu pōla ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷil muktā eṉum peyar peruvaḻakkil kāṇappaṭāmiyāṉum ivvuṇmi viḷakkamākum.
kapāṭapuramum atic cūḻnta pāṇṭi nāṭum iraṇṭāmūḻi yiṟutiyil mīṇṭum oru kaṭal kōḷāl aḻintateṉpatum, ataṉ piṟakē kumari mutal cōṇāṭṭaḷavuḷḷa taṟkālap pāṇṭi nāṭṭi yamittu viyik kariyil tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal eṉum maturiyit talinakarākkip pāṇṭiyar āṇṭu vantaṉar eṉpatum, yāvarum aṟinta ceytiyākum. icceytiyē muṟkāṭṭiya mullikkalip pāṭṭuk kuṟippākum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷukku muṉṉirunta paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ teṟkelli kumariyāṟeṉpatum, ak kumariyē piṟa teṉṉāṭṭit tamiḻnaṭṭiliruntu piritta pērelli yeṉpatum, paḵṟuḷi mutaliya piṟa tamiḻ nilattaṭaṅkiya pakuti yaṉittum aḻinta kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkē yiruntaṉavēyām eṉpatum, orutaliyāt tuṇiyappaṭum uṇmikaḷākum.

24. tamiḻum tamiḻarum
tamiḻar tāmē puṟakkaṇittup poruḷāk koḷḷāta tamiḻ taṟkālam nākarikap piṟa moḻiyāḷarāl eḷḷappaṭiṉ iḻukkā mō? taṟceyyār tiṉan tāḻu nili māṟṟa aṟivaṟintu āḷviṉi mēvāt tamiḻariṭit taḷarātu tamiḻaṇaṅku taḻiva teṅṅaṉ? uḷḷamilār, uṭimiyilār, vaḷḷiya meṉṉuñ cerukkumillāt tamiḻp periyār palartām, innāḷ tam iḻivit tamiḻkkēṟṟi ataṉaḻivu cūḻukiṉṟār. aṟivu ēṟṟam aṭivataṟkum, tamiḻ moḻiyē iṭiyūṟeṉṟu iyampukiṉṟār. mēṉāṭṭār payiṉṟuvarum putika naṟpalkaliyum, iṟimi nalattuṟivakiyum tamiḻmoḻiyāṟ peṟavoṇāveṉap pēcukiṉṟār. intiyar ōriṉak kuṭimi yuṇarvuṟṟu, oṟṟumi peṟṟu uyartaṟkut tamiḻ vaḷarttal taṭiyeṉavum muḻaṅku kiṉṟār. maṭimēl tamiḻar mayakkattāl tam muṉṉōr kuṉṟātakuṭi viḷakka maṟantu, taṅkaḷ aṭiminilit taḷarvāl tam uram aṟṟu āḻntu uṟaṅkupavar tamakkiraṅkāta avar kūṟṟip poruṭ paṭuttal tavaṟāmaṉṟō?
kumari mutaṟ paṉivarimaṭ ṭuṟupavarkaḷ oru moḻi koṇṭaṉṟi oṉṟākār eṉpataṟku niyāyamuṇṭō? pala moḻikaḷ nilavu taṉip perunāṭu ulakil orumiyuṭaṉ perumi yuṟṟu uyarak kāṇpōm. aikkiya nāṭukaḷ eṉṉum aḻakiya peyarpaṭittu nākarika ulakattiṉ nāyakamāyt taliniṟkun taṉiyaracil, pala moḻika ḷuṭiyārē kuṭimiyāḷpavar. mēlip puttulakattiṉ vaṭataliyiṟ kāṉaṭa naṉṉāṭṭiṉilum, āṅkilam paṟaṅkiyumeṉṟiru moḻikaḷum maruvi vaḷarntiyaṅkak kāṇpōm. vāṉavar nāṭeṉa vaḻaṅkuñ cīṉar  paḻam perunāṭṭil toṉṟu toṭṭu iṉṟaḷavum palvēṟu nalmoḻikaḷ payilukiṉṟār. iṉṉa peru nāṭaṉittum, nilapparappuṅ kuṭipperukkum intiya nāṭataṟku eṉittuṅ kuṟiyāmi koṇṭuṭiyavēṉum, oṟṟumi nalluṇarvu niti palvēṟu moḻipayilvār nitam vaḷarkka, vaḷam perukic ceḻumiceṟi cerukkuṭaṉē nilavak kāṇpōm. eṉavē, orumiyaraciṉ uyarvukkut tamiḻāṭci innāṭṭil taṭitaraṟku niyāyamilli.
iṉi, mēṉāṭṭiṟ putikamum, putukkalikaḷ palavum eḻun tulavu muṉṉar nilavi niṉṟa moḻikaḷi, ammoḻiyuṭi yār puttaṟivup perunaṟavukku utavāta kalaṉkaḷeṉap puṟak kaṇittup putu moḻikaḷ tēṭi avar vaḷarkkavilli. aṟivu urimikku uṟiviṭamāyk karutivarum ikkāla āṅkilamum, cermaṉiyam paṟaṅkiyeṉum moḻikaḷum, putukkaliyum, puttaṟivum vaḷarntu avaṟṟi vaḷañceyumuṉ, ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamu millāta iḷañciṟiya kuḻavimoḻi niliyil neṭuṅkālam avi nilaviyuḷavām. puttaṟivi nattiya ammoḻiyuṭiyār tam moḻiyil aṟivutarum putukkalikaḷ pukutti nalampaṭit tallāl, kuṟi moḻi mēṟ kūṟivittut tammiḻivu pēṇupaḻi pūṇṭārallar. avar moḻipōlallāmal, aṟiveṭṭāt tollariya paḻamiyoṭu ākkavaḷam peṟṟilaṅkun tamiḻiṉilē putukkalikaḷ tavaḻntilaki vīṟupeṟat tamiḻ nalamum kalivaḷamu moruṅkōṅkun takiyavākum. ōrntuṇarār iti maṟuppar; uṇmi iḵtē!
nāṭṭiṉ nalam vaḷarppataṟku nāṭṭiṉar evvakuppiṉarum uyartal vēṇṭum. moḻi vaḷappam illārpāl aṟivākkam uyarvatilli. akaṉṟāḻnta aṟivoṭpam, āṉṟa aṟanūṟṟiṭpam, māṇṭa maṉiyākkamoṭu vēṇṭum aracēṟṟamum uṟṟu, makkaḷ perumi peṟuvataṟkellām muṉṉar mutalutavuvatu moḻi vaḷappam. kutali moḻi miḻaṟṟiya taṅkulamoḻipōl aṟivu, tiru āṟṟal vaḷam perukkutaṟku itaṉil piṟitilli yākum. tolvaḷamum ilakkaṇa naṟcevvikaḷum amiyāta ciṟumoḻikaḷāmēṉum, tammoḻiyē matinuṭpam nūlākkam cāṉṟāṇmi camutāya veṟukkiyelām taḻivikkat takkatākum nākarikameṉ ṟeḻuntatu. aṉṟuḷa centamiḻuṭiyār taḷar vaṭiyiṉ, avariyaliṉ cōrvallāl moḻiyiṭiyōr kuṟiyumilli. centamiḻt tāyum avaḷīṉṟa cila kutalimoḻi mārumaṉṟi nam nāṭṭuḷa moḻikaḷaṉittum vaṭaperu moḻiyiṉ vaḻivanta toṭarpuṭiya. avi payilvār perumpālum piṇakkaṟṟa moḻi yorumiyiyal iṇakkam pēṇaṟ pālar. tamiḻ uḻuvaṟṟollurimi vaṭamoḻiyē vaḻutti niṟkum. kuṟiyāta vaḷamuṭiya vaṭa collum, piḻiyāta kaṉṉimiyār tamiḻc collum elliyaṟu tolli mutal iṉṟaḷavu maruvi yoṉṟi nilavum namatu intiya nāṭṭilētāṉ moḻi yīcalpōṟ paṟantu tūṟṟumiḻi vēkkam vantatiṉṟu. moḻipēṇār muṉṉēṟṟa vēkanala muṇaroṇātār. nāṭṭiṉalam namatākkam iraṇṭilli, oṉṟēyākum. perunalam nammavar vēṇṭiṉ teruvellān tamiḻ muḻakkañ ceyya vēṇṭum.

25. iruperuṅ kalikaḷ
pakuti 1
ovvoru makkaḷ vakuppiṉ uyirppaṇpum ataṉ kali yaṟivil naṉku veḷippaṭum. ataṉ meymmit tōṟṟam ataṉ ilakkiyat tokutiyiṟ kāṇappeṟum. muṉṉēṟṟam muḻutum taḷarvillāta iyaṟkip paṇpukaḷākiya paci, pāṟpirivu ivaṟṟāl untappeṟṟu niliyuṟukiṉṟatu. atutāṉ, ēṟṟam peṟṟa makkaḷ tokutiyuḷ kātaṟ kāppiyaṅkaḷākavum pōrp puṟappāṭṭuk kaḷākavum avarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ malarkiṉṟatu. paḻaṅkālat tamiḻar kaliyaṟiviṉ varalāṟutāṉum ippotu vitikkup puṟaṉaṭiyaṉṟu. uṇmiyil, tamiḻccaṅkap paḻam pāṭalkaḷ allatu nūṟṟokutikaḷ yāvum akam, puṟam eṉṉum iruperum pirivil amintamiyi nām kāṇkiṟōm. akam eṉpatu kātal uḷḷap pāṅkiṉiyum ataṉ piṟa cārpukaḷiyum veḷip paṭuttamiyum ceyyuṭkaḷi muṟiyāl varintu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. atupōṉṟu puṟam eṉpatu illiṉ puṟattaṉa ākum makkaḷ ūtiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷiṉ muḻu elli yip paṟṟiyatēyām.
camakiruta ilakkiyap parappuḷ, ceyyuṭkaḷ yāvum, tarma, artta, kāma, mōkṣaṅkaḷāya nāṉku puruṭārttaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟiyō cilavaṟṟiyō viritturippaṉa ākum. ivviti tāṉ camakirutac ceyyuḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ ellāp pakuppukkaḷukkuḷḷum naṉku vaṟpuṟuttap peṟṟum kiyāḷap peṟṟum uḷḷatu. āṉāl, mikap paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ellām tamiḻ marapiṟkiṇaṅka, akam puṟam eṉṉum irupiriviṉ kaṇṇēyē amintiruppatu namakku muṟpaṭat tōṉṟukiṟatu. muṟiyāṉē ecceyyuḷum puruṭārttap pakuppil oṉṟuḷ allatu cilavaṟṟuḷ aṭakkap peṟalām. ivvāṟē paḻantamiḻc caṅkap pāṭṭukkaḷum innūl pakuppuṭpaṭṭup poruntit tōṉṟumāṟu vakippaṭuttalām. āṉāl, makkaḷ ūtiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum, oruvaṉ aka vāḻkki allatu taṉinili vāḻkki, puṟa
vāḻkki allatu potunili vāḻkki eṉṉum iru pakup
puṭpaṭuttum tamiḻar vaḻakkē peritum iyaṟkiya tākavum aṟiviṟkum poruttamāṉatākavum tōṉṟukiṟatu. camakiruta nāṟpirivukaḷ aḷavi neṟiyāṟ ciṟitu kuṟipāṭuṭiyaṉavākat tōṉṟum, eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, kalvi allatu kaliyaṟivu taṉṉiyalpil innāṉkaṉuḷ muṟṟum muṟiyē aṭaṅkātatākavum, arttam allatu kāmatti yokka ittuṇi karutaṟkurittākavum mutaṉmittākavum koḷḷap peṟavilli. itaṟku māṟāka, intac camakirutap pirivukaḷ tamiḻilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷ uyartturikkum iruperum pirivukaḷuḷ māṟupāṭiṉṟi eḷitil aṭaṅkum. ituvē, tamiḻar, camakirutakkārar ivarkaḷtam ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ tāmtām koḷḷum paḻamiyāṉa mutal vēṟu pāṭākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē āriyar, tamiḻar ākiya ivviru pērāṟukaḷum talimaṇantaṉa. annāḷtoṭṭu ik kalappu iṭiyaṟātu iṉitāy oḻukit tamiḻakameṉṟa teṉṉāṭṭic ceḻippikkiṉṟatu. pārata pūmiyiṉ paṇṭik kaliyaṟivup pērāṟukaḷ iraṇṭum kalappuṟum taliyiṭamākiya pirayākiyāka nam tolkāppiyar nūlik koḷḷalām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kariya teḷinīr yamuṉiyum, piṟitoṉṟu ceḻippikkum ceṅkaluḻik kaṅkiyum ām eṉalām. ivviru kaliyaṟiviṉ mutaṟ kalappiṉippaṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum ittolkāppiya nūl uṭiyatu. ikkalappu nikaḻnta īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉuḷḷanām, ivviraṇṭum kalakkum pirayākikkup paṉṉūṟu kaṟkaḷukku appāl attaṉippērāṟṟiṉ karikkaṇṇuḷḷa kāḷikkaṭṭattil niṟpavarāvōm. ivviru kaliyaṟivukaḷiṉ kalappiṉik kāṇavirumpiṉ, nām, pirayākikkuc ceṉṟu, ivviru nīrk kalappiṉ valiyiṉiyum, pōkkiṉiyum aṟiyalām. uruvaka naṭiyoḻittut teḷivuṟak kūṟiṉ, intap paḻamiyāṉa iraṇṭu intiyak kaliyaṟivukaḷiyum oruvāṟu aṟiya virumpu vāroruvar tamiḻp peru nūlākiya tolkāppiyattil nuḻintu ārāyak kaṭavar. tamiḻaruṭiya kaliyaṟivut tiṟaṅkaḷit tīṭṭiya ivvaṇṇap paṭam tolkāppiyatti ārāyntataṉālākiya payaṉēyām.
paṇṭit tamiḻar nūlkaḷil terinta aḷavil, mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālattē tamiḻariṉ, kali aṟivu teḷiviṉatākavum, taṉ valiyatāyum, amitiyatāyum, taṉṉiyalpiyatāyum iruntamiyi nām innūlārāycciyāl eḷitiṉ aṟiyalām. āṉāl, āriyar kaliyaṟivō itaṟku maṟākak kalappiṉatāyum, iṇakkiṉṟik kavartaluṭiyatāyum, taṟceṟippuṭiyatāyum palliyalpiṉatāyum iruntatākat tōṉṟum.
īṅku ivaṟṟic ciṟitu viḷakkamākat teḷivikkayāṉ muyalvēṉ. tamiḻc ceyyuḷiṉ akapporuḷ allatu ilvāḻkkip pakutiyi nām mutaṟkaṇ eṭuttuk koḷvōm. akkālat tamiḻar ēṟṟamikka cīrmiyum, amivuṭiya ilvāḻkkiyum potuvāḻkkiyum uṭiyarāyi runtaṉareṉa mikka ārāycci muyaṟciyiṉṟit terintukoḷḷalām. tamiḻakameṉṟa tamnāṭṭuḷ paṇṭutoṭṭu oṉṟupaṭṭa tokutiyarāy avarkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar. tamiḻar eṉpār, cintiyar allatu tirāviṭa vakuppiṉareṉṟum, intiyāvukku veḷināṭṭiliruntu vaṭakiḻakku vaṭamēṟkuk kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyāy uṭpukuntaṉa reṉṟum, tamakkup piṉṉarp pōnta āriyappaṭikaḷiṉāl teṟkiṉkaṇ turattappaṭṭaṉareṉṟum kūṟic cila paḷḷip puttakaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷi vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉarāka nāṭṭa muyalkiṉṟaṉa. ik kūṟṟukkaḷ tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ teḷiyak kāṇum akaccāṉṟu kaḷukku muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭaṉavē. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, ivarkaḷ vaṭakkiṉkaṇ vēṅkaṭa mali yākiya elliyiṉatum, teṟkiṉkaṇ kumarimuṉi, ilaṅkit tīvam ivaṟṟoṭu toṭarpuṟṟutteṉkaṭaluṭ paranta nilap parappiṉatum ākiya nāṭṭil toṉmakkaḷāka vāḻntaṉareṉa viḷaṅka urikkiṉṟaṉa. kalittoki nūliluḷḷa oru pāṭal, tamiḻakattiṉ teṉpakutinilam kaṭalkōṭpaṭṭoḻiya āṇṭirunta makkaḷ vaṭapāṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉareṉṟuteḷivikkiṉṟatu. tamiḻar yāṇṭum teṉṉāṭṭut toṉmakkaḷ eṉṟum, vaṭavāriyar tamiḻakattil pukkapōtu avarkaḷit tamiḻar oruṅkuṭaṉpaṭṭu ēṟṟup pēṇik kuṭiyēṟṟuvittaṉar eṉṟum avarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl nām aṟiyalām. āriyari vaṭavāriyar eṉṟum, avar moḻiyākiya camakirutatti vaṭamoḻi eṉṟum, ammoḻiyōṭu māṟupaṭṭa tamiḻ moḻiyit teṉmoḻi yeṉṟum paṇṭinūlkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṭal varippiṉatāya tamiḻnāṭṭuḷ neytalnila makkaḷ iyalpoṭu poruntiya amitiyāṉa vāḻkkiyip peṟṟiruntaṉareṉa yāṉ vaṟpuṟutturik
kiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻar pakivarāl alippiṉṟi amitiyāṉa vāḻvu peṟṟiruntaṉar. ōrōrkāl tamiḻkkuṭi marapu vāḻkkikku ottavāṟu tammuḷ porutoḻil viḷittaṉaraṉṟip piṟarpāṟ pōr muṉippu, nāṭukavar viḷimi ivaṟṟiṟ karuttilarāyiruntaṉar.
āṉāl, vaṭavintiyāvil āriyarō eṉiṉ, tām kuṭi
yēṟiya varippil, tammoṭu eṉṟum māṟupaṭṭa paṇṭik kāṉuṟi māntariṭiyē oru ciṟu cīrmiyuṟṟa kuḻuviṉarā yiruntaṉar. ātaliṉ, āriyar, tam kuṭiyēṟṟa varippakattē, cīrmi yillāta anāriyak kāṉuṟi māntarkaḷip porutu veṉṟu tam cīrmiyiyum kalic celvattiyum, nilimiyiyum valiyuṟuttuk kāttu vaḷaṉuṟuttal mutaṟkaṭaṉāyiṟṟu. ātaliṉ, āriyaṉ eṉṟum porutoḻiliṉāyp piṟar nāṭu kavar niṉippiṉaṉāy iruttalkaṭappāṭāyiṟṟu. itaṉ payaṉāy āriyaṉ taṉṉāṭṭamitiyiyum ilvarum iṉpaṅkaḷiyum koḷḷutaṟku iṭaṉ ilatāyiṟṟu. avaṉukkut taṟkāppē taṉikkaṭaṉāyiṟṟu. innili, avaṉ taṉ kuṭumpattip pōr
puriyum valiyuṭiyatākkic camutāyat tokutiyil oru pakutiyāyk kūṭṭuṟavuk kuṟiyāyc camikkat tūṇṭiyatu. avaṉ eṉṟum pōr ceyvār palari vēṇṭiṉaṉ; ātaliṉ, pakimi koṇṭa kāṉuṟi māntarkaḷiyum muruṭarkaḷiyum porutaṟkuriya āṇmakkaḷip perukkutaṟku virumpiṉaṉ. inniliyil āriyaṉ taṉ kātaliṉpattiṉum āṇmakkaṭ pēṟṟilēyē peritum karuttuk koṇṭaṉaṉ. ātaliṉ, avaṉ kātalukkaṉṟi makap
pēṟṟiṉ poruṭṭē maṇam ceytaṉaṉ. akkālattu āriyaṉukkut taṉ kuṭittūymi pōṟṟalē kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu. ātaliṉ, taṉ kula
makaḷiriyēṉum anniya, piṟa kuṭippeṇkaḷiyēṉum vili
koṇṭāyiṉum kaṭaṉ koṇṭāyiṉum maṇam purintu kuṭit
tūymi kākka virumpiṉaṉ. kuṭumpa niliyil kātaṟkuriya perumiyi avaṉ ciṟitum karutavilli. ātalāl, āriyaṉ maṇattit taṉ kuṭiccaṭaṅkākak koṇṭu, taṉ kūṭṭunilik kuṭiyip perukkit taṉ pōrttiṟatti vaḷarttut taṉ pakivarip porutaḻikkak karutiṉaṉ eṉa nām aṟiyalām.
itaṟku māṟākat, tamiḻaṉō eṉiṉ, amitiyum celvamum aḷaviṉṟit tanta taṉṉāṭṭakattē pakivarāl alippiṉṟi amitiyāy vāḻntaṉaṉ. ātaliṉ, avaṉ illiṉpa vaḷattil kavaṉam celuttiṉaṉ. iṉpuṟu maṇavāḻkkikku mutalatāka aṉpu niliyē mēleṉak karutiṉaṉ. tamiḻar maṇamuṟik kellām irutali yorumi yaṉpum kātaṟ kiḻamiyum yāṇṭum uriyaveṉat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum. āṉāl, taruma cāttiraṅkaḷō eṉiṉ, maṇakkap pukum maṇamakaṉ, taṉ maṇamakaḷi maṇattukku muṉ kāṇutaṟkut tāṉum iṭam vēṇṭila. avaṉ avaḷit tirumaṇak kālattil tāṉ kāṇavum, tāṉ maṇakka nērntavaḷpāl, taṉ kātalic celuttak kaṟkavum vēṇṭum. tamiḻaṉ iṉpa vāḻvu vēṇṭiyōṉātaliṉ, tāṉ kātalitta
vaḷiyē maṇakka nāṭiyatōṭu, maṇattiṟku muṉpē, taṉpāl avaḷ otta kātaluṭiyāḷ eṉpati aṟiyavum nāṭiṉaṉ. tamiḻaṉ taṉ makaviṉpāl, āriyaṉiṟṟāḻa aṉpu ceytaṉaṉeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. tamiḻaṉpu taṉ kātalipāl muntuṟṟu muṉittu nilittatu. aṉpotta kātalar īṉṟa makavu taṉ kātaṟ peṟṟōrāl iyalpāy aṉpu ceyyap peṟṟateṉka. curuṅkac colliṉ āriyaṉ makappēṟu kāraṇamāka maṇantaṉaṉ. paṇṭit tamiḻaṉō ammakaviṉi īṉṟa tāyiṉpāṟ koṇṭa kātalāl avaḷpāṟ peṟṟa makkaḷik kātalittaṉaṉ. tamiḻkkuṭit talimakaṉ, iṉpa ilvāḻkkiyē ciṟantateṉṟu karuti vaṟpuṟut tiṉamiyāl, irupāṟ kātaṟ kiḻamiyē paṟṟukkōṭāka iru pālarum maṇantu vāḻneṟiyi nilipeṟac ceytaṉaṉ. mētaku maṇamuṟiyil kātal maṇaṅkaḷukkumuṉ kātalittalum irantu piṉṉiṟṟalum iyalpāṉa nili eṉak karutiṉaṉ. ivvērūṉṟiya tamiḻar uḷappāṭē paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ evaṟṟilum miḷir
vaṉavāyiṉa. kaṟpu neṟikku iṉṟiyamiyāk kaṭaṉākak kaḷaveṉṉum kātalum irantu piṉṉiṟṟalum vaṟpuṟutturikkappaṭṭaṉa. tolkāppiyamum tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvum tamiḻar uḷap
pāṭṭi irupālar maṇattiṟkum iṉṟiyamiyākkaṭappāṭāka aṟintu vilakkalākāt tiruneṟiyākak koṇṭu oḻuka vaṟpuṟuttu
vaṉavām. ittakiya tamiḻar kātalneṟit tirumaṇattiṟku muṉ valiyuṟṟiruntamiyāl, taṅkaḷ paṇṭi aṟanūlkaḷiṉ vilakkilā oḻukka vitiyāka itaṉi amittuk kūṟiṉar. aṉṟiyum cama
kirutap peruntirukkāppiyattic ceyyuḷ naṭiyil peyarttuk koṭutta perum pulavarākiya kamparum, tamiḻar uḷappāṭum, vaḻakkum, māṟāk kōṭpāṭumākiya itaṟku iṇaṅkavē kati nikaḻccikaḷiyum amippukkaḷiyum māṟṟiyurittaṉar. tamiḻilakkiyam vaṟpuṟuttuvatu kaṭimaṇattiṟku muṉpuḷḷa kātalē yaṉṟu; ataṟkup piṉpum vāḻkkiyiṟ kuṟivilatum iṟavātatumākiya kātalēyām. akapporuḷ nūluḷ, tuṟiyeṉap peyar peṟṟa mikavuyarnta amippukkaḷ ivvirupālār maṇattiṟkum nilitta kātaṟ kaṭaṉip paṟṟi valiyuṟutti urikkiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, nilimāṟāk kātalākiya iruvayiṉottal oṉṟiṉik kāṇka. vāḻnāḷ muḻutum kiḻattiyāṟ kiḻavōṉ kātal eṉṟum tūyatāyiruttal vēṇṭuma. eṉṟum niliyātu āṇṭōṭu taḷarcciyāl aḻivuṟuvaṉavākiya talimakaḷ iḷamiyum vaṉappum eḻilu mutaliyavaṟṟāl iḵtu ēṟpaṭuvatu aṉṟu. kātalariruvariṭiyē eṉṟum nilitta iṟavā aṉpum, kaḻiyāp paṟṟum, māṟā aḷiyum, kuṟiyāp pārāṭṭum ākiya paṇpukaḷē kātal maṇattiṟku uriyaṉavākum. maṇavāḻkki eṉṉum nāṟcatura maṉikku aṉpum, maṭamum, cāyalum, iyalpumākiya nāṉkum iṉṟiyamiyā mūlittūṇkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa. aṉpeṉpatu oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāmi; maṭam eṉpatu oruvar kuṟṟam oruvar aṟiyāmi. ivikaḷē kātalar iruvarpālum nilipeṟaṟkuriya uyar paṇpukaḷeṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻar tirumaṇattukku evvakic caṭaṅkum iṉṟiyami yātaṉavākap paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ karutavilli eṉpataṟkup paṟpala cāṉṟukaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ uṇṭu. mutaṟkaṇṇum eññāṉṟum uḻuvalaṉpē maṇattiṟkum il vāḻkki iṉpattiṟkum uriyatoṉṟāka iruntatu. poruntā taṉavum ceyaṟkiyum ākiya āriyar vaḻakkukaḷiyum, muṟimi kaḷiyum, tamiḻariṉ kātal paṟṟiya vaḻakkukaḷ, kuṟikkōḷ kaḷōṭum iṇakki vēṟupāṭu aḻittu oṉṟākkac cila pirāmaṇa pirāmaṇīya ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷ ellām viḷakka vēṇṭā veḷippaṭiyāy uḷḷaṉa. eṇ vakippaṭṭa āriyar maṇamuṟikaḷuḷ perumpāṉmiya, maṇa meṉum peyarkkē poruntātaṉa. picāca rāṭcacak kūṭṭaṅkaḷi maṇameṉak kūṟuvatu, meymalivum moḻiyiṟ paḻippuriyum ākum. pōṟṟiyurikkappaṭum kantaru vantāṉum nilitta maṇam aṉṟu. ōr āṇmakaṉum peṇmakaḷum orukāl tammuḷ talip peytaliyē kuṟippatākum. piṉṉar avar uṟavu nilipeṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum niṉipputtāṉum aṅku illi. ivvōr kālik kātal veḷippāṭṭit tamiḻar kaḷavu muṟiyākiya kiḻattimāṭ ṭuyirkkātal muṟikku oppiṭṭurippatu peruṅ kuṟṟamēyātaliṉ aṅṅaṉam karutik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟukkaḷi vilakkal vēṇṭum. āriyaruṭiya maṟṟa aivaki maṇaṅkaḷum maṇattiṟku muṉpu uriya kātaṟ kiḻamiyik kuṟittu oṉṟum vitippaṉavalla. uṇmiyil āriyar maṇamuṟikaḷil kātal mutaṟ poruḷāka vitikkappaṭavē illi. kātaṟ kiḻamikku iṭaṉiṉṟāyiṉum avar maṇamuṟikaḷ oppuṭiyaṉavāka amikkap peṟṟup pukaḻappaṭukiṉṟatum yāvarum aṟintatē.
pakuti 2
ituvari irupālar kātaṟ kiḻamikku nilikkaḷaṉ ākiya akapporuḷpaṟṟik kūṟiṉām. carita kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakattil, ikkātal maṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟu ilvāḻkki yiṉpattiṟ kaṉintaṉa. paṇṭit tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ maṟṟoru tōṟṟamākiya puṟapporuḷip paṟṟip paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟik kāṇpām. īṇṭu, maṟṟoru periya iyaṟkit tūṇṭutalākiya pacit tuṉpu aṭippaṭiyāka uṇṭāya eḻucci, pālviḷiyālākiya kātaṟ paciyoṭu vaḷarvitta nākarikatti nōkkuvām. muṉṉēṟṟam eṉpatu irupālār maṇattoṭum ilvāḻvoṭum niṉṟuviṭuvatilli. kuṭumpaṅkaḷ kuṭit tokutikaḷākap perukukiṉṟaṉa. iṉavāḻvum camutāya vāḻkkiyum irupāṟ kalappiṉ payaṉāy amivaṉavē. pāl viḻivāl eḻuntu iyalpiṟ palatiṟattavāy aḻivuṟum uyirkaḷiṉ etir viṉikaḷāy oḻiyātu, makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil nilitta uyarnta paṇpukaḷāy aṭivē vaḷarvatākum. nilitta paci, pāl viḻiviṉ  veḷippāṭṭōṭu cērntakāli, atu makkaḷ taṅkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattit tiruttic camikkavum virivuṟap paṇṇavum utavukiṉṟatu. vaṭa vāriyarum teṉṟamiḻarum tammuḷ talip peytaṟku muṉpē, virinta camutāya vāḻkkiyiyum araciyal muṟikaḷiyum vaḷampaṭa amittuk koṇṭōrāvar. caṅka nūlārāycci, āriyar kalappiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ camutāyak kuṭivāḻkki iyalpukaḷi aṟintu koḷḷavutavum. annūṟ ṟokutiyiṉ ārāycci kaṅki varippil āriyar amittuk koṇṭa camutāya muṟikaḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa iyalpukaḷiyum, kāviri, paḵṟuḷi varippukaḷil tamiḻar amittuk koṇṭa tiṇiyoḻukkaṅkaḷiyum muṟiyē vēṟupaṭuttiya avvavar cūḻalkaḷiṉ viṉiyāṟṟalkaḷi veḷippaṭak kāṭṭuvatākum. kuṭumpa camutāya amippukaḷil amitiyuṭiya tamiḻarkuṭikaḷum, porutiṟaṉuṟṟa āriyar kuṭikaḷum vēṟupaṭṭa cuḻalkaṭkum kālaṅkaṭkum iyaṉṟavāṟu aminta vēṟuvēṟu iyalpukaḷiyum viḷivukaḷiyum eytiṉar eṉpatik kāṇac cāṉṟukaḷ pala ip paṇṭi nūṟ ṟokutikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaṉ taṉ kuṭumpattip porutaṟkuriya niliyatāka amittuk koḷḷat tūṇṭukōl etuvum ilaṉ āyiṉaṉ. ēṉeṉiṉ, āriyaṉip pōṉṟu pakittuc cūḻnta piṟar kuṭikaḷip porutu oḻikkumāṟu etirpārkka nēravilli. ātaliṉ, avaṉ taṉ kuṭumpattit taṉṉilittāka vaḷarvittāṉ. tamiḻaṉtaṉ uṭimikaḷ, tāṉē īṭṭiyavēṉum taṉ peṟṟōr vaḻip peṟṟaṉavēṉum avaṟṟil avaṉ putalvar allatu peyararkaḷ ōr urimiyum nāṭutal ceyyār. āriyar valiyum uṟutippāṭum peṟṟa kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa nalaṉkaḷi virintu eytiṉar. tamiḻarō, taṉi muyaṟci vaḷarccip payaṉkaḷiyum taṉi makkaḷiṉ uyirttiṟa malarcciyiṉiyum aḻuttamuṟa eṭuttu iyampiṉar. kuṭumpac cottukkaḷ muḻuvatiṉum kūṭṭāḷikaḷiṉ kūṭṭūtiyak karuttē āriyar akkālattavar iruppiṭa iyalpukaḷukku ēṟpak karutit tāṉ kātalittāḷi maṇantu vāḻntamiyiṉ kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa niliyik karuttuṭ koḷḷavilli. avaṉ putalvarum peṟṟōr neṟipēṇit tattam taṉimaṉi vāḻkkiyiṉi amittuk koṇṭaṉar. itaṉ payaṉāyt tamiḻar kuṭumpaṅkaḷ taṉi niliyavāyum āriyar kuṭumpaṅkaḷ kūṭṭuniliyavāyum amintaṉa.
pakuti 3
ikkaṭṭuriyi muṭippataṟku muṉ camutāya araciyal vāḻkkit tiṟatti nōkkutal oṉṟē eñci uḷḷatu. tamiḻariṉ pōrttuṟi ilakkaṇamum pōr aṟavitikaḷum tamiḻakak kali vāḻviṉ puṟattiṇi allatu puṟavoḻukkattiṉ ciṟanta pakuti yākum. makkaḷiṉ ip puṟavāḻkkit tuṟiyilum ivviru vakuppāriṉ camutāya vāḻviṟ kuriya cūḻalkaḷ, kāla iyalpukaḷ, avaṟṟil muṟiyē tamakkuriya kuṟikaḷip poṟittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟāl ivviru vakuppāriṉ kali kalantu ottu vaḷarnta pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum viyappukkiṭaṉāy mutirntu tiruntiya avartam paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil avvavar aṟap paḻiya taṉi iyalpukaḷik kāṇalākumāṟu amin tuḷḷaṉa. caṅkanūṟṟokuti muḻutum ivvaki ārāyccikku vaḷamoṭu virinta iṭantaruva tōṭu ārāycci muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭum pala māṇavarkkum perum payaṉi aḷippatum ākum. ituvariyil pukuntaṟiyā maṇikkuvic curaṅkaṅkaḷākiya paḻantamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ ārāycci viṉiyit tūṇṭutalum viḻiviṉi eḻupputalumē eṉ karuttu. ātalāṉ icciṟiya kaṭṭuriyil ataṉ pala tiṟaṅkaḷiyum yāṉ virikka villi. mēṉ muyaṟciyum, mikka tiṟamum uṭiyār appaṭat tiṉi muṟṟa muṭitteḻutikkoḷḷac cila kuṟivarikaḷimaṭṭum yāṉ iṅkut taruvēṉ.
muṉṉiya pakutikaḷil ivviru vakuppāriṉ vēṟupaṭṭa cūḻalkaḷākiya vāḻkki, kalikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kūṟik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. akkālattu āriyar tam vakuppiyum kuṭumpattiyum illattiyum kāttu vaḷarppatil iṭiyīṭillāp pōr muyaṟci vēṇṭiṉar. annākarikak kūṭṭattāri oḻittut tam uyarnta nākarikatti nilippaṭuttap pōr mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ivviṭiyīṭillāp porutoḻil vāḻkki, pōrē pōṟṟat takkatu eṉṟa karutti avarkku uṇṭākkiṉamiyāl, atu taṅkaḷ camutāya vāḻkkiyil appōr viṉimikkuriya amippukkaḷi nilippaṭutti vaḷarkkat tūṇṭiṟṟu. ataṉāl, kāla aṭivil uyarmatippum urimikaḷum peṟṟa pōr viṉiyāḷar vakuppu oṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. cattiriyarkaḷ itaṉāl araca pataviyip peṟṟataṉōṭu tammil uyarnta pirāmaṇar mutalāya piṟa cātiyāriṉ pārāṭṭukkaḷiyum peṟalāyiṉar. cattiriyaṉ pirāmaṇaṉi yoppak kurukkaḷ patam eytāviṭiṉum pala kālaṅkaḷil āciriya urimikaḷi antaṇa rōṭu paṅkiṭṭu aṉupavittaṉaṉ. vētap pakutikaḷum pala āraṇṇi yaṅkaḷum pōrviṉiyāḷarākiya irācaṉṉiyarkaḷiṉ pukaḻcci kaḷip palapaṭap pōṟṟi  urikkiṉṟaṉa. āriyar taruma cāttiraṅkaḷ, pōrttiṟam, ataṉ ciṟappukkaḷ ivaṟṟi upatēcit tatuvum aṉṟi, cattiriya cātiyāriṉ nītik kūppāṭākavum uyarttik kūṟiṉa. toṭarntu veṟṟikoḷ toḻili cattiriyarkku vitittaṉa. cattiriya vakuppiṉarkkup piṟar nilam kavar peṭpum, viḷivil veṟṟi vēṭkiyum, maṭaṅkā uḷḷa māṇpum uriyaṉavākak kaṟpittaṉa. ivvāriyar kōṭpāṭu avar tam nīti nūlkaḷil cattiriyar iṭiyaṟātu mēṟkoḷḷattakkatāka uyarttik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; tiruviṉiyākak kiyāḷap peṟṟatu.
paṇṭi āriyar tammoṭu māṟupaṭṭa vakuppiṉari oḻikkumāṟu iṅṅaṉam iṭiyīṭu iṉṟip porutal mēṟ koṇṭārāka, caṅka kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar piṟar nāṭu kavar viruppatti vaḷarkkak karutavilli. vaṭakkiṉkaṇ āriyaṉ vakuppuṇarcci valiyāl mēṟkoṇṭu porutāṉākat teṉṉāṭṭakattē tamiḻaṉ taṟkāppiṉ poruṭṭum māṉattiṉ poruṭṭumē porutoḻil pūṇṭāṉ. tammavar vāḻviṟkum vaḷa vaḷarccikkumākap pōril muṉippuk koṇṭa āriyar kōṭpāṭṭiṟku māṟākat tamiḻar ikal mēṟkoṇṭu pōrp poruṭṭāyp porutal ātaritturiyātu tam aṟanūl vitikaḷil iḻitturittaṉar. uṇmiyil tamiḻaṉ pōriṉiyum, pōr veṟṟiyiyum, taṉ illam, māṉam, ivaṟṟik kākkum poruṭṭē mēṟkoṇṭaṉaṉ. tirukkuṟaḷ, tolkāppiya vitikaḷukkuk kālattāṉum poruḷāṉum naṭiyāṉum peritu ottuk kūṟum nūlākum. tamiḻp pāṇiṉiyār vititta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku vaḷḷuvaṉār ciṟanta ilakkiyam amittuḷḷār. tolkāppiyaruḷ varintuṇarttiya aṟiviyal aṟattuṟikaḷit teḷittuk kāṭṭuṅ kaṇṇāṭiyē tirukkuṟaḷām. paḻantamiḻk kaliyaṟivup paṭattiṟkut tolkāppiyar variyum kuṟiyum vakukkat tirukkuṟaḷ vaṇṇamum varivum vaḷamuṟat tarukiṉṟatu. muṉṉatil ēṟpuc caṭṭamum uṭaṟkūṟum amiyat tirukkuṟaḷil uṭampum uṭaliyal nūṟporuḷum varintuḷḷaṉa. vaḷḷuvaṉār kuṟaḷil 86, 87-ām atikāraṅkaḷākiya ikal, pakimāṭci ivikaḷ, paṇṭi āriyar pōr, pōr maṉappāṉmikaṭkum etirāyt tamiḻmakkaḷ koṇṭa nīti maṉappāṉmiyi valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikal allatu pōr viruppu ekkālattum vari viṉṟi iḻitturikkappaṭṭatu. itu vaciyuṭit tīmiyeṉa maṟukkappaṭṭatu; eṉiṉum, tamiḻaṉ āṇmiyil pēṭiyum, eṇmi viruppuṭip peṇmiyaṉum allaṉ. ēṉeṉiṉ, taṉ maṉikkāppu allatu taṉ māṉattiṉ  māṇpu karutiyapōtu kaṭipōkumaṭṭum pōr ceytaṟku nimirkiṉṟāṉ. tamiḻ makaḷir vīrantāṉum caṅkap pulavar pāṭum takiyatākiya poruṭṭuṟi yāka irukkiṟatu. paṇṭit tamiḻakattu āṇpālarum peṇ pālarum ottu virumpip paṅkeṭuttu mēṟkoṇṭa aṟappōr viṉiyāl ākum veṟṟiyiyum pukaḻiyum avarkaḷ matittup pōṟṟiyati appāṭalkaḷ veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭi intiyāviṉ kaliyaṟivu malinta ivviru peru vakuppiṉarum, kātal, pōrviṉik kōṭpāṭukaḷiṟ koṇṭa vēṟṟumikaḷ, avvavar vāḻkki nōkka vēṟupāṭukaḷukkum palatiṟappaṭṭa camutāya amippu, araciyal amippu, aṟaneṟi amippu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷukkum tamtam iyalpiṟ kāraṇaṅkaḷāyiṉa. tamiḻaṉ piṟappāṉē naṉṉampikkiyāḷaṉ; makiḻvum kaḷippum nirampiyōṉ; vāḻkkiyiṉ nalliyalpik kaḷippuṭaṉ kikkoṇṭu nilavulakil iṉiya kuṭumpa vāḻvi virumpi aṟattoṭu poruntum iṉpa vāḻkkiyiyē eṉṟum vēṇṭiṉavaṉ. āriyaṉō veṟuppiyal viruppiṉaṉ; palvaki iṉpaṅkaḷiyum veṟuttōṉ; vāḻkki vilakkat takka tuṉpam eṉa veṟuttu, vāḻkkit tuyaraṅkaḷukkellām tuṟavē naṉmarunteṉa upatēcittu, maṟuvulaka nōkkik kaṭip piṭittōṉāvāṉ.

26. paṭṭiṉappālit talivaṉ...?
cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉik kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār pāṭiya paṭṭiṉappāli eṉṟu iṉṟaḷavum pulavar palar kūṟuvatik kēṭkiṉṟōm; kiṭṭiya nūṟcāṉṟaṉittum, paṭṭiṉappālit talivaṉ yārāyiṉumākalām, orupōtum karikālaṉāka māṭṭāṉ- eṉa veḷḷiṭip perumalipōl viḷakkak kāṇpām. eṉiṉum, karikālaṉē paṭṭiṉappāli koṇṭu makiḻntu, atip pāṭiya pulavaṉukkup patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ paricaḷittāṉ eṉum koḷki pulavariṭiyē vērūṉṟi vaḷarntaṭintu paṭarntuvarap pārkkiṉṟōm. ati maṟuppōr pulavar palar nakippiṉukku āḷāvatilum aiyamilli.
taṟkāla akattiyar eṉat tamiḻp pulavar pārāṭṭum takkār makāmakōpāttiyāya cāminātiyaravarkaḷ patippitta puṟanāṉūṟṟil pāṭappaṭṭōr varalāṟṟil cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippip pārppōm.
muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār pāṭiya porunarāṟṟuppaṭikkum, kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār pāṭiya paṭṭiṉappālikkum talivaṉ ivaṉē. paṭṭiṉappāli kēṭṭuk kaṭiyalūr urut tiraṅkaṇṇaṉārukkup patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ paricaḷittā ṉeṉṟu kaliṅkattup paraṇi irāca pārampariyam 21-ām ceyyuḷāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
iṉi, avarkaḷ veḷippaṭuttiya pattuppāṭṭu mukavuriyil paṭṭiṉappāli ... ... ... ... taliviyip pirintuvārēṉeṉac celavaḻuṅkik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟākac cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷat tāṉik kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār pāṭiyatu eṉṟum eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
iḵtu ip perumpulavar tam karuttākat tarum kuṟippu. ittuṇivu tām koṇṭataṟkuk kaliṅkattup paraṇit tāḻici yoṉṟi mēṟkōḷākat tarukiṉṟārkaḷ. at tāḻici varumāṟu:-
tattu nīrvarāṟ kurumi veṉṟatum
		taḻuvu centamiḻp paricil vāṇarpoṉ
	patto ṭāṟunū ṟāyi rampeṟap
		paṇṭu paṭṭiṉap pāli koṇṭatum
itu cōḻar kuṭiyiṟ kaliṅkap pōr veṟṟi paṟṟiya paraṇiyil avveṟṟi koṇṭa vicayataraṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu irā
cēntiraṉiṉ muṉṉōr perumi pārāṭṭik kulamuṟi kiḷattum irācapārampariyap pakutiyil varukiṟatu. itil paṇṭu paṭṭiṉap pāli koṇṭu patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ paricu tantatik kūṟiyatu tavira, avvāṟu ceytavaṉ karikālaṉ eṉṉum kuṟippuc ciṟitum illi. irācapārampariyat tāḻicikaḷ apayaṉ eṉṟa vicayataraṉiṉ muṉṉōrāṉa cōḻa maṉṉar palarāṟṟiya aruñ ceyalkaḷi niṉintu puṉintu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
kamala yōṉi mutalākavaru muṅkaḷ marapiṟ
		kāvaṉ maṉṉarka ḷākivaru kiṉṟa muṟiyil,
aravaṇittuyilumātimutalākavapayaṉ...
		taraṇi kāva laḷavum
collum ivvaralāṟṟiṉ toṭakkattil karikālaṉ kūṟap paṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉukku nāratar ivvaralāṟu kūṟukiṟār. itil, cēraṉic ciṟiviṭuttuk kaḷavaḻippāk koṇṭa ceṅkaṭ cōḻaṉ ceytikkup piṟakē paṭṭiṉappāli koṇṭa paricu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
muṭiccōḻarkuṭi vēntar mūvar muṟiyē aṭiparava, avaraviyi aṇi ceyta kūttar yātta ulā mūṉṟum karikālaṉ peruvāḻvum, avaṉ piṉṉē ceṅkaṇāṉ āṭciyum piṟaḻāta variciyilē pēcak kāṇpōm. paraṇiyilum paṭṭiṉappālip paricu ceṅkaṇāṉ ceytikkup piṉṉarē varukiṉṟatu.
cōḻarukku niṟipulami yamiccariṉum neruṅki niṉṟa kūttarum, cēkkiḻārum karikālaṉ paṭṭiṉappāli koṇṭa kati kūṟukilar. iḷaṅkōvum karikālaṉ pālikkup paricaḷitta kuṟippetuvum kūṟukilar.
iviyaṉittum pālikkup paricu tantu pukaḻ koṇṭavaṉ yāreṉṟu kūṟavilli. ākavē, paṭṭiṉappāli koṇṭu paricaḷitta perumi vicayataraṉ muṉṉōruḷ oru cōḻaṉatu eṉpatu maṭṭumē paraṇittāḻici kūṟuvataṉṟi accōḻaṉ karikālaṉ eṉpataṉi eṉittaḷavum cuṭṭavilli. ātaliṉāl, paṭṭiṉappālit talivaṉ karikālaṉeṉpatu piṟacāṉṟāl teḷiyap peṟṟāl ipparaṇittāḻiciyum aṉṉavaṉic cuṭṭuvatāy eṇṇa iṭam tarutal kūṭum. aṉṟi, atu taṉṉaḷavil poṉ tantōṉ yāreṉṟu cāṉṟu utavat takkataṉṟu. pālikkup paricaḷitta cōḻaṉ yāreṉpataṉip piṟa nalla cāṉṟukaḷāl ārāya vēṇṭuvatu meyvirumpum pulavar kaṭaṉ.
inniliyil, paṭṭiṉappāliyum cilappatikāramum mūvarulāvum kūṟāta koḷki yoṉṟit tamiḻp pulavar mēṟkoḷḷa nērntatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyari uccimēṟ koṇṭataṉ payaṉākum. paranta nūṟpayiṟci niṟinta urikāraruṭ ciṟanta aṭiyārkku nallārik kalviyāl oppārāyiṉum, aṭiyārkku nallārpōl aṭakkamiṉṟi, eppōtum yāvarum kūṟāp putiyaṉa puṉiyum perumita mikunta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar paṭṭiṉappāli yīṟṟil tirumāvaḷavaṉ eṉpataṟkut tiruviṉ perumiyi yuṭiya karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ eṉṟu eḻutiyavuriyē pulavari maruṭṭum ik koḷkiyiṉ vitiyum viḷivum koḻukompu mākum. paṭṭiṉap pāli kāṭṭum tirumāvaḷavaṉ karikālaṉ eṉpataṟku ivar kūṟuvatoḻiya vēṟu cāṉṟetuvum yāṇṭum kāṇutaṟkilli. ivar, tamkūṟṟēetiyumniṟuvappōtiyātaravāmeṉaXtuvatallāmal,ãṟacāṉṟumnvaṇṭumvṉavṇṇavoṇṇāïyalpuṭiyār.M¡»nah® bpayarKtaliyaFṟippiṉṟitjmcrikaḷiṟgṟpalabcayyuṭkaḷiïvarvṭutjḷuvatujmTṟṟukkuMtaravufṭṭutaṟkaṉṟu,jmflvipgrappieṭṭutaṟkēyāmvṉpatu,mṭiyārkkuellārgrimēlaḻakarnpālvāruriyōṭuïvarurikaḷieṭuniliyilxttunnākkiṉvḷitilbvaḷiyākum.ïÅ¥ paṭṭiṉappālit talivaṉ karikālaṉ eṉṉum ivar kūṟṟu aṭiyaṟṟa neṭumaramāvati iṅkuc ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
mutalil, paṭṭiṉappāli pāṭiya uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyiyum ākkiyavar. ippāṇāṟu toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉip pāṭiyatu. iḷantiriyaṉ karikālaṉukkup piṉ āṇṭa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ makaṉeṉṟu mūvarulākkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum ippulavar iyaṟṟiya akam-167, kuṟuntoki 352-ām pāṭṭukkaḷum ivarik karikālaṉukkup piṟkālattavarākak kāṭṭum. porunarāṟṟuppaṭi pāṭiya muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyārum maṟṟum karikālaṉip pukaḻum veṇṇik kuyattiyārum karuṅkuḻalātaṉārum karikālaṉukkuk kālattāṟ pintiyār yāriyum pāṭāmiyum īṇṭuk karutaṟkuriyatu.
iraṇṭāvatākap paṭṭiṉappāli kūṟum pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ varalāṟu karikālaṉip paṟṟiya porunarāṟṟuppaṭi varalāṟṟōṭu evvakiyilum eṉittaḷavum ovvāmal muṟṟilum muraṇi vēṟupaṭuvatum, ivviru pāṭṭum tattam talivaṉukku vevvēṟu varalāṟu kūṟuvatum orumuṟi paṭippiṉum uṭaṉē viḷaṅkum uṇmikaḷām. pēr piṟappu iḷami aracurimi āṭci vāḻkki pōr veṉṟi peṟṟōr cuṟṟam uṟṟār ceṟṟār mutaliya ovvoṉṟilum karikālaṉum paṭṭiṉappālit talivaṉum (ivvirupāṭṭuṭit talivarum) tammuṭ ciṟitum ovvāta iruvēṟu cōḻar eṉpati āṟṟuppaṭiyum paṭṭiṉap pāliyum aṭitōṟun terivikkum muraṇpāṭṭāl iṉitu kāṇpōm.
immuṟiyil nōkkuṅkāl toṭakkattil tōṉṟuvatu itu:-karikālaṉ pērum piṟappum pōrum perumiyum porunarāṟṟuppaṭi palkālum palaviṭattum palapaṭap pukaḻntu pārāṭṭukiṟatu. paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ muṉṉūṟṟōraṭi muḻutum turuviṉum karikāṟ cōḻaṉ peyarēṉum piṟappu pōr veṉṟi mutaliya varalāṟṟuc ceytiyēṉum kāṇoṇātu. muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār,

ivvāṟu ulakameytiya miku pukaḻ veṟṟiyip pālip pāṭṭu ataṉ talivaṉ pōrttiṟam palapaṭap pakarum pakutiyiṟ ciṟitum kuṟiyāmi karutaṟkuriyatu. karikālaṉ pukaḻ karutip pāṭil vakiyoṭu veṇṇikkaḷattamar kaṭanta avaṉ vākip pāṭāṇ tiṟatti uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār maṟattal kūṭumō?
aṉṟiyum, neṭuñcēralātaṉum aṭuntiṟal māṟaṉum karikālaṉ porutu veṉṟa pakivarāvar. immuṭiyuṭiyār iruperu vēntar perumitamaṭakkiya taṟukaṇmi karikālaṉatu iṉṉōr tōlviyi ikaḻntu maṟantu ciṟunāṭṭuk kuṟumaṉṉaṉ iruṅkōvēḷ maruṅku cāya etirttu veṉṟatoṉṟimaṭṭum paṭṭiṉappāli pārāṭṭukiṟatu. iruṅkōvēḷ, taliyālaṅ kāṉattu neṭuñceḻiyaṉ veñcamar muṟukki nalvala maṭakkiya eḻuvaruḷ oruvaṉ; (akam-36. puṟam 19-36. pāṭṭukkaḷip pārkka) kapilarāl pāṭap peṟṟavaṉ kapilarum neṭuñceḻiyaṉum karikālaṉukkuk kālattāṟ pintiyavar. karikālaṉoṭu porutu puṟappuṇ koṇṭavaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ. neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkut tōṟṟuc ciṟiyiṭappaṭṭavaṉ yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēral. iccērar iruvarum muṉpiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa kālattavar. itaṉāl porunarāṟu paṭṭiṉappālip pāṭṭut talivar iruvēṟu cōḻarāvar; avarkaḷip peṟṟōrum ceṟṟārum vevvēṟaracarāvar; pōrkkaḷamum vevvēṟām eṉpaṉa vellām viḷakkamākum.
iṉi, ivviru pāṭṭum vevvēṟu vakiyil tammuḷ muraṇpaṭak kūṟum varalāṟukaḷiyum ciṟitu ārāyvōm. karikālaṉ piṟappum iḷamiyum maṟapparum perumiyum ciṟappiṉil oppum tēṭutaṟkariya ceruvaṭuntiṟalum tāmakkaṇṇiyār āṟṟup paṭiyil tarum paricu ituvām:-

karikālaṉ uruvap paḵṟē riḷiyōṉ ciṟuvaṉ: uruvap paḵṟēriḷiyōṉ payaṅkeḻu māmaḻi... vayamāṉ ceṉṉi (puṟam. 266) ceññāyiṟṟuk kaviñaṉ (puṟam-4). peruvaḷa muṭiya puṉal nāṭṭi aruntiṟa lōṭāṇṭu māṇṭa iccōḻaṉ iṟakka, avaṉaracurimittiru appōtu avaṉ kōmaṉivi vayiṟṟiliruntu piṉpiṟanta karikālaṉik karuvilaṭintatu. itaṉāl, ivaṉ tāy vayiṟṟiruntē (cōṇāṭāḷum) tāyameytiṉaṉ eṉat tāmakkaṇṇiyār vāymi kūṟiṉār. ivaṉ cīrum ciṟappum tiruvuṭic cōḻavaḷanāṭṭāṭciyum piṟantatu mutalē piṟaṅkit tikaḻntaṉaṉ. ataṉāl piṟantu tavaḻ kaṟṟataṟṟoṭṭuc ciṟanta naṉṉāṭu cekiṟ koṇṭu nāṭoṟum vaḷarppa (poruna. 137-138) eṉṟu āṟṟuppaṭi pukaḻkiṉṟatu.
